## [2026-04-22] comparison | Stripe Link vs PayPal Fastlane: Checkout Acceleration

- Created: `wiki/comparisons/stripe-vs-paypal-checkout-acceleration.md`
- Covers: UX architecture (enhancement vs orchestrator), token lifecycle, subscription two-step flow with verified sandbox API logs, customization constraints, integration fit by scenario
- Findings from sandbox verification (PayPal sandbox, Orders API):
  - Step 1: `single_use_token` + `store_in_vault: ON_SUCCESS` → `vault_id: 0jt88057hw1618155`, `status: VAULTED`, $1.00 captured
  - Step 2: `vault_id` + `stored_credential` (no browser, no Fastlane SDK) → $9.99 captured
  - Correction: `payment_type: "RECURRING"` is required in Orders API `stored_credential`; `usage_pattern` belongs to the Payment Method Tokens v3 API, not Orders API
- Updated: `wiki/concepts/paypal-vault.md` — noted `stored_credential` schema correction
- Updated: `wiki/index.md` (added Comparisons section with first entry)
